## 3.2 Adjective → Adverb (-mente) — Esercizio 2 pattern
Rule: take the FEMININE singular form of the adjective + -mente. If the adjective ends in -e, just add -mente directly. If it ends in -le or -re, drop the final -e first.

| **Aggettivo** | **Avverbio**    | **English**                | **العربية** |
|---------------|-----------------|----------------------------|-------------|
| chiaro        | chiaramente     | clearly                    | بوضوح       |
| attento       | attentamente    | attentively / carefully    | بانتباه     |
| lento         | lentamente      | slowly                     | ببطء        |
| gentile       | gentilmente     | kindly / politely          | بلطف        |
| silenzioso    | silenziosamente | silently                   | بصمت        |
| rapido        | rapidamente     | quickly                    | بسرعة       |
| paziente      | pazientemente   | patiently                  | بصبر        |
| preciso       | precisamente    | precisely                  | بدقة        |
| perfetto      | perfettamente   | perfectly                  | بشكل مثالي  |
| distratto     | distrattamente  | distractedly               | بشرود       |
| facile        | facilmente      | easily (drops final -e)    | بسهولة      |
| regolare      | regolarmente    | regularly (drops final -e) | بانتظام     |
| forte         | fortemente      | strongly                   | بقوة        |
| veloce        | velocemente     | fast                       | بسرعة       |

# A2 · PART 4 — GRAMMAR EXPLAINED
## 4.1 AVERE vs ESSERE — the two engines of Italian grammar
Both verbs do TWO jobs: (1) they're normal verbs with their own meaning, and (2) they're the 'auxiliary' (helper) verb that builds the Passato Prossimo. Mixing these up is the \#1 A2 mistake — here's both jobs, side by side.

**AVERE — 'to have' — used for:**

| **Uso**                              | **Esempio**                                      | **English**                                          |
|--------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------|
| Possesso (to have/own)               | Ho un libro.                                     | I have a book.                                       |
| Età (age)                            | Ho 25 anni.                                      | I am 25 years old. (lit. 'I have 25 years')          |
| Sensazioni fisiche (physical states) | Ho fame / sete / freddo / caldo / sonno / paura. | I'm hungry / thirsty / cold / hot / sleepy / scared. |
| Ragione / torto                      | Hai ragione. Hai torto.                          | You're right. You're wrong.                          |
| AUSILIARE — Passato Prossimo         | Ho mangiato. Ho lavorato. Ho letto.              | I ate. I worked. I read. — used with MOST verbs      |

**ESSERE — 'to be' — used for:**

| **Uso**                      | **Esempio**                 | **English**                                                                                                        |
|------------------------------|-----------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Identità (identity)          | Sono Ahmed.                  | I am Ahmed.                                                                                                         |
| Descrizione (description)    | Sono stanco. Sei alto.      | I am tired. You are tall.                                                                                          |
| Luogo / posizione (location) | Sono a Roma.                | I am in Rome.                                                                                                      |
| Nazionalità / professione    | Sono tunisino. Sei ingegnere. | I am Tunisian. You are an engineer.                                                                                  |
| AUSILIARE — Passato Prossimo | Sono andato/a. Sono nato/a. | I went. I was born. — used with movement / change-of-state / reflexive verbs; participle AGREES in gender & number |

**How to know which auxiliary to use in Passato Prossimo: almost everything takes AVERE by default. ESSERE is the exception, reserved for this specific group of verbs (memorize this list — it's often called 'la casa di essere'):**

| **Italiano**             | **English**          | **العربية**           |
|--------------------------|----------------------|-----------------------|
| andare                   | to go                | يذهب                  |
| venire                   | to come              | يأتي                  |
| arrivare                 | to arrive            | يصل                   |
| partire                  | to leave/depart      | يغادر                 |
| entrare                  | to enter             | يدخل                  |
| uscire                   | to go out            | يخرج                  |
| salire                   | to go up / get on    | يصعد                  |
| scendere                 | to go down / get off | ينزل                  |
| tornare / ritornare      | to return            | يعود                  |
| restare / rimanere       | to stay/remain       | يبقى                  |
| stare                    | to stay/be           | يبقى                  |
| essere                   | to be                | يكون                  |
| nascere                  | to be born           | يولد                  |
| morire                   | to die               | يموت                  |
| crescere                 | to grow              | ينمو                  |
| diventare                | to become            | يصبح                  |
| cadere                   | to fall              | يسقط                  |
| riuscire                 | to succeed/manage    | ينجح                  |
| piacere                  | to like/please       | يعجب                  |
| dispiacere               | to be sorry          | يأسف                  |
| bastare                  | to be enough         | يكفي                  |
| mancare                  | to be missing/miss   | يفتقد                 |
| succedere                | to happen            | يحدث                  |
| costare                  | to cost              | يكلّف                  |
| tutti i verbi riflessivi | all reflexive verbs  | كل الأفعال الانعكاسية |

*Remember: with ESSERE, the past participle agrees with the subject like an adjective (-o masc.sing / -a fem.sing / -i masc.pl / -e fem.pl). With AVERE, the participle never changes.*

> ***Es:** Marco è andato. / Maria è andata. / Loro sono andati.* — Marco went. / Maria went. / They went. — participle changes
>
> ***Es:** Marco ha mangiato. / Maria ha mangiato.* — Marco ate. / Maria ate. — participle never changes with avere

## 4.2 Passato Prossimo — completed past actions
Formation: AVERE or ESSERE (present tense) + past participle.

- Regular participles: -ARE → -ato · -ERE → -uto · -IRE → -ito

- See 4.1 above for the full ESSERE-verb list and the agreement rule.

> ***Es:** Marco è andato dal medico.* — Marco went to the doctor. (ESSERE, agrees with Marco)
>
> ***Es:** Maria ha ricevuto un messaggio.* — Maria received a message. (AVERE, no agreement)
>
> ***Es:** Noi siamo andate al mare.* — We (all women) went to the sea — participle takes -e for feminine plural.

## 4.3 Imperfetto — background, habits, description in the past
Used for: ongoing situations, repeated/habitual actions, descriptions (people, weather, age, feelings), and 'while' actions.

Endings: -ARE → -avo/-avi/-ava/-avamo/-avate/-avano · -ERE → -evo... · -IRE → -ivo...

- Irregular: essere → ero, eri, era, eravamo, eravate, erano

- Irregular: fare → facevo · dire → dicevo · bere → bevevo · tradurre → traducevo

> ***Es:** Quando ero piccolo, avevo paura dei temporali.* — When I was little, I was afraid of storms.
>
> ***Es:** Faceva freddo e pioveva.* — It was cold and it was raining. (description)

*Passato Prossimo vs Imperfetto together: the Imperfetto sets the scene, the Passato Prossimo is the event that interrupts it.*

> ***Es:** Studiavo quando hai chiamato.* — I was studying when you called.

## 4.4 Futuro Semplice — what will happen
Regular: -ARE and -ERE verbs share endings; -ARE verbs change the a → e before adding them (parlare → parler-). -IRE keeps the i.

-erò / -erai / -erà / -eremo / -erete / -eranno (-IRE: -irò / -irai / -irà ...)

- Irregular stems (same stem is reused in Condizionale): essere→sar-, avere→avr-, andare→andr-, fare→far-, dire→dir-, dovere→dovr-, potere→potr-, volere→vorr-, venire→verr-, vedere→vedr-, sapere→sapr-, rimanere→rimarr-, tenere→terr-, bere→berr-.

> ***Es:** Domani pioverà tutto il giorno.* — Tomorrow it will rain all day.
>
> ***Es:** Il prossimo mese inizierò un nuovo lavoro.* — Next month I will start a new job.

## 4.5 Condizionale Presente — 'would' / polite requests / hypotheticals
Same irregular stem as Futuro + these endings: -ei / -esti / -ebbe / -emmo / -este / -ebbero.

> ***Es:** Se potessi, direi al mio amico di non comprare quel computer.* — If I could, I would tell my friend not to buy that computer.
>
> ***Es:** Vorrei parlare con il direttore.* — I would like to speak with the manager. (polite request)
>
> ***Es:** Se avessi tempo, studierei di più.* — If I had time, I would study more.

## 4.6 Modal verbs (potere / dovere / volere) in Passato Prossimo
Auxiliary depends on the infinitive that follows: use ESSERE if the following verb normally takes essere, otherwise AVERE (in practice, AVERE is very common and always safe at A2 level).

> ***Es:** Non ho potuto finire il lavoro.* — I wasn't able to finish the work.
>
> ***Es:** Sono dovuta partire presto.* — I had to leave early. (essere-verb 'partire' → agreement)

## 4.7 Adverbs ending in -mente
Placed after the verb, or at the beginning/end of a sentence for emphasis. See the full table in Part 3.2.

> ***Es:** Sara parla chiaramente.* — Sara speaks clearly.
>
> ***Es:** Probabilmente arriverà domani.* — He will probably arrive tomorrow.

## 4.8 Irregular past participles — quick scan
fare → fatto · dire → detto · essere/stare → stato

vedere → visto · leggere → letto · prendere → preso

scrivere → scritto · aprire → aperto · chiudere → chiuso

chiedere → chiesto · rispondere → risposto · decidere → deciso

mettere → messo · perdere → perso · rompere → rotto

bere → bevuto · venire → venuto · nascere → nato

morire → morto · rimanere → rimasto · offrire → offerto

## 4.9 Articulated prepositions (preposizioni articolate)
| **Prep.** | **+ il**  | **+ lo**      | **+ la**      | **+ i**   | **+ gli**     | **+ le**      |
|-----------|-----------|---------------|---------------|-----------|---------------|---------------|
| di / da   | del / dal | dello / dallo | della / dalla | dei / dai | degli / dagli | delle / dalle |
| a         | al        | allo          | alla          | ai        | agli          | alle          |
| in        | nel       | nello         | nella         | nei       | negli         | nelle         |
| su        | sul       | sullo         | sulla         | sui       | sugli         | sulle         |

## 4.10 Tense clue words — decide the tense before you conjugate
| **Espressione**                  | **English**            | **العربية**            | **Tempo**                     |
|----------------------------------|------------------------|------------------------|-------------------------------|
| ieri / ieri sera                 | yesterday / last night | أمس / أمس مساءً         | Passato Prossimo              |
| la settimana scorsa              | last week              | الأسبوع الماضي         | Passato Prossimo              |
| l'anno scorso                    | last year              | العام الماضي           | Passato Prossimo / Imperfetto |
| una volta                        | once / one time        | ذات مرة                | Passato Prossimo              |
| quando ero piccolo/a             | when I was little      | عندما كنت صغيراً        | Imperfetto                    |
| da bambino/a                     | as a child             | في طفولتي              | Imperfetto                    |
| sempre / spesso                  | always / often         | دائماً / غالباً          | Imperfetto (habits)           |
| mentre                           | while                  | بينما                  | Imperfetto                    |
| domani                           | tomorrow               | غداً                    | Futuro Semplice               |
| la settimana prossima            | next week              | الأسبوع القادم         | Futuro Semplice               |
| il mese prossimo                 | next month             | الشهر القادم           | Futuro Semplice               |
| più tardi / in futuro            | later / in the future  | لاحقاً / في المستقبل    | Futuro Semplice               |
| se avessi / se potessi           | if I had / if I could  | لو كان لدي / لو استطعت | Condizionale                  |
| ogni giorno / di solito          | every day / usually    | كل يوم / عادة          | Presente                      |
| adesso / ora / in questo momento | now / right now        | الآن / في هذه اللحظة   | Presente Progressivo          |

## 4.11 Presente Progressivo — 'I am \_\_\_-ing' (stare + gerundio)
Used for an action happening right now, in this exact moment — not a habit. Compare: Studio italiano (I study Italian — in general, a habit) vs Sto studiando italiano (I am studying Italian — right now, this moment).

**Formation: STARE (presente) + gerundio.**

| **Persona** | **stare** |
|-------------|-----------|
| io          | sto       |
| tu          | stai      |
| lui/lei/Lei | sta       |
| noi         | stiamo    |
| voi         | state     |
| loro        | stanno    |

**Gerundio endings:**

| **Terminazione** | **Gerundio** | **Esempio**          |
|------------------|--------------|----------------------|
| -ARE             | -ando        | studiare → studiando |
| -ERE             | -endo        | leggere → leggendo   |
| -IRE             | -endo        | dormire → dormendo   |

**Irregular gerunds:**

| **Verbo** | **Gerundio** | **English**    | **العربية**         |
|-----------|--------------|----------------|---------------------|
| fare      | facendo      | doing / making | يفعل / يصنع (حالياً) |
| dire      | dicendo      | saying         | يقول (حالياً)        |
| bere      | bevendo      | drinking       | يشرب (حالياً)        |
| tradurre  | traducendo   | translating    | يترجم (حالياً)       |

> ***Es:** Sto studiando italiano.* — I am studying Italian. — أنا أدرس الإيطالية الآن
>
> ***Es:** Sto leggendo un libro.* — I am reading a book. — أنا أقرأ كتاباً الآن
>
> ***Es:** Cosa stai facendo?* — What are you doing? — ماذا تفعل الآن؟

*Bonus use: the gerund alone (without stare) links two simultaneous actions.*

> ***Es:** Camminando, ho incontrato Maria.* — While walking, I met Maria.
>
> ***Es:** Mangio guardando la TV.* — I eat while watching TV.

## 4.12 Pronomi Diretti e Indiretti — direct & indirect object pronouns
Direct object answers 'cosa?/chi?' (what/whom) — it receives the action directly, no preposition in the full sentence. Indirect object answers 'a chi?/a cosa?' (to whom/to what) — the full sentence has 'a' before it.

**Diretti (replace 'cosa/chi' — no preposition):**

| **Pronome** | **English**  | **العربية** |
|-------------|--------------|-------------|
| mi          | me           | ني / ـي     |
| ti          | you          | ك           |
| lo          | him / it (m) | ه           |
| la          | her / it (f) | ها          |
| ci          | us           | نا          |
| vi          | you (pl)     | كم          |
| li          | them (m)     | هم          |
| le          | them (f)     | هنّ          |

**Indiretti (replace 'a + persona/cosa'):**

| **Pronome** | **English** | **العربية** |
|-------------|-------------|-------------|
| mi          | to me       | لي          |
| ti          | to you      | لك          |
| gli         | to him      | له          |
| le          | to her      | لها         |
| ci          | to us       | لنا         |
| vi          | to you (pl) | لكم         |
| gli / loro  | to them     | لهم         |

> ***Es:** Vedo Marco. → Lo vedo.* — I see Marco. → I see him. — أرى ماركو ← أراه
>
> ***Es:** Parlo a Marco. → Gli parlo.* — I speak to Marco. → I speak to him. — أتحدث إلى ماركو ← أتحدث إليه
>
> ***Es:** Scrivo ai miei amici. → Gli scrivo.* — I write to my friends. → I write to them. — أكتب لأصدقائي ← أكتب لهم

*Position: before a conjugated verb (Lo vedo); attached to the end of an infinitive with the final -e dropped (voglio vederlo); attached to a gerund (vedendolo); attached to an informal imperative (guardalo!).*

## 4.13 Ne e Ci — two small words that replace a lot
NE replaces 'di + noun' — used for quantities and for referring back to a topic already mentioned ('of it/of them/about it').

> ***Es:** Quanti libri hai? Ne ho tre.* — How many books do you have? I have three (of them). — كم كتاباً لديك؟ لدي ثلاثة (منها)
>
> ***Es:** Parliamo del film? Sì, ne parliamo.* — Shall we talk about the film? Yes, let's talk about it. — هل نتحدث عن الفيلم؟ نعم، نتحدث عنه

CI replaces 'a/in + place' — 'there' — and is also fused into certain fixed verbs (pensarci, crederci, metterci).

> ***Es:** Vai a Roma? Sì, ci vado.* — Are you going to Rome? Yes, I'm going there. — هل تذهب إلى روما؟ نعم، أذهب إلى هناك
>
> ***Es:** Credi a questa storia? Sì, ci credo.* — Do you believe this story? Yes, I believe it. — هل تصدق هذه القصة؟ نعم، أصدقها

# A2 · PART 5 — ORAL EXAM PREP (2 past + 1 future question)
*Structure every past answer like the reading text you were given: Imperfetto for background/feelings → Passato Prossimo for what actually happened, in time order, linked with quindi, poi, dopo, per questo motivo, alla fine.*

**Domanda 1 (Passato) — Cosa hai fatto lo scorso weekend?**

Sample answer:

> ***Es:** Lo scorso weekend sono rimasto/a a casa perché ero un po' stanco/a. Sabato mattina ho pulito la casa e poi ho studiato per l'esame di italiano. Nel pomeriggio ho giocato online con i miei amici, come faccio di solito. La sera ho guardato un film. Domenica il tempo era bello, quindi sono andato/a a fare una passeggiata e ho fatto qualche fotografia. Alla fine è stato un weekend tranquillo ma piacevole.* — Last weekend I stayed home because I was a bit tired. Saturday morning I cleaned the house and then studied for my Italian exam. In the afternoon I played online with my friends, as I usually do. In the evening I watched a movie. Sunday the weather was nice, so I went for a walk and took some photos. In the end it was a quiet but pleasant weekend.

*Key phrases to reuse: sono rimasto/a a casa, ho studiato, ho giocato con i miei amici, il tempo era bello, sono andato/a a..., alla fine.*

**Domanda 2 (Passato) — Raccontami un ricordo d'infanzia / una cosa che è successa la settimana scorsa**

Sample answer (childhood memory — uses Imperfetto for habits):

> ***Es:** Da bambino abitavo in una piccola città sul mare con la mia famiglia. Ogni estate andavamo al mare e giocavamo sulla spiaggia tutto il giorno. Avevo paura dell'acqua profonda, ma mio padre mi insegnava sempre a nuotare. Un giorno, quando avevo dieci anni, ho nuotato da solo per la prima volta: è stato un momento molto importante per me.* — As a child I lived in a small town by the sea with my family. Every summer we would go to the sea and play on the beach all day. I was afraid of deep water, but my father always taught me to swim. One day, when I was ten, I swam alone for the first time: it was a very important moment for me.

*Notice the pattern: Imperfetto (abitavo, andavamo, avevo) for the repeated/background part, then ONE Passato Prossimo verb (ho nuotato) for the specific event that stands out.*

**Domanda 3 (Futuro) — Cosa farai il prossimo weekend / quali sono i tuoi progetti futuri?**

Sample answer:

> ***Es:** Il prossimo weekend studierò per il mio esame e lavorerò un po' in negozio. Sabato sera, però, uscirò con i miei amici e giocheremo insieme. Domenica farò una corsa la mattina, come faccio spesso. In futuro spero di trovare un lavoro interessante e di parlare bene l'italiano.* — Next weekend I will study for my exam and work a bit at the shop. Saturday evening, though, I will go out with my friends and we will play together. Sunday I will go jogging in the morning, as I often do. In the future I hope to find an interesting job and speak Italian well.

*Key phrases to reuse: il prossimo weekend..., studierò, lavorerò, uscirò con i miei amici, in futuro spero di + infinito.*

**Last-minute checklist before you sleep**

- Essere-verbs in passato prossimo agree in gender/number — say them out loud with 'io sono andato' AND 'io sono andata' so your ear catches it.

- Futuro & Condizionale share the same irregular stem — if you know one, you know the other.

- When a question says 'la settimana scorsa' → Passato Prossimo. 'Da bambino' / 'sempre' → Imperfetto. 'Il mese prossimo' → Futuro.

- For PARTE 4 (Produzione Scritta, 80-100 parole): open with a time marker, use 2-3 passato prossimo verbs in order, one imperfetto for context, and a closing sentence with 'alla fine' or 'in generale'.

# A2 · PART 6 — CEFR ROADMAP: A0 → B1 (grammar with examples)
*This is cumulative: A1 assumes A0, A2 assumes A1, B1 assumes A2. Read this when you have spare time — it's context, not last-minute cramming material.*

## 6.0 — A0 (Assoluto Principiante)
The absolute basics — often taught before 'official' A1 begins.

- Alphabet & pronunciation: c/g are hard before a,o,u and soft before e,i.

> ***Es:** casa (house) — hard 'k' · cena (dinner) — soft 'ch'* — gatto (cat) — hard 'g' · gelato (ice cream) — soft 'j'
>
> ***Es:** gn = 'ny' sound: gnocchi. gl(+i) = 'ly' sound: famiglia. sc(+e/i) = 'sh': pesce.* — Double consonants lengthen the sound: casa (house) vs cassa (box/till).

- Gender & plural of nouns: masc. -o→-i, fem. -a→-e, either gender -e→-i.

> ***Es:** libro → libri (book → books)* —
>
> ***Es:** casa → case (house → houses)* —
>
> ***Es:** cane → cani, notte → notti (dog/night → plural)* —

- Subject pronouns: io, tu, lui/lei, noi, voi, loro (see Part 1.1).

- Essere & avere in presente, learned as vocabulary before the 'system' is explained.

> ***Es:** Sono italiano. Ho un libro.* — I am Italian. I have a book.

- Definite/indefinite articles: il/lo/la/i/gli/le, un/uno/una (see Part 1.3).

- Basic adjective agreement.

> ***Es:** Il libro è bello. La casa è bella.* — The book is beautiful. The house is beautiful. (adjective matches noun gender)

- Numbers 0–20, greetings.

> ***Es:** Ciao! Buongiorno! Come stai?* — Hi! Good morning! How are you?

- C'è / ci sono — 'there is / there are'.

> ***Es:** C'è un libro sul tavolo. Ci sono due libri.* — There is a book on the table. There are two books.

- A first handful of -ARE verbs learned by rote (not yet the full conjugation system).

> ***Es:** Parlo italiano.* — I speak Italian.

## 6.1 — A1
Presente indicativo is now taught as a full SYSTEM — see Part 1.4 for the complete -ARE/-ERE/-IRE endings, plus the irregulars: essere, avere, andare, fare, stare, dire, dovere, potere, volere, venire, uscire, sapere.

- Possessive adjectives — agree with the thing owned, not the owner.

> ***Es:** il mio libro / la mia casa* — my book / my house

- Demonstratives: questo (this) / quello (that).

> ***Es:** Questo libro è nuovo. Quella casa è vecchia.* — This book is new. That house is old.

- Simple prepositions (not yet articulated): in, a, di, da, con, su, per, tra/fra.

> ***Es:** Vivo in Italia. Vado a scuola. Sono di Tunisi.* — I live in Italy. I go to school. I'm from Tunisi.

- Interrogatives.

> ***Es:** Dove abiti? Quanti anni hai?* — Where do you live? How old are you?

- Negation: non + verb.

> ***Es:** Non parlo francese.* — I don't speak French.

- Reflexive verbs in presente.

> ***Es:** Mi chiamo Ahmed. Mi alzo alle 7.* — My name is Ahmed. I get up at 7.

- Piacere — basic use.

> ***Es:** Mi piace il caffè. Mi piacciono i libri.* — I like coffee. I like books.

- Imperativo informale (tu).

> ***Es:** Vai! Aspetta! Guarda!* — Go! Wait! Look!

- Passato prossimo — introduced, mostly with avere, plus a few common essere-verbs, without deep focus on agreement yet.

> ***Es:** Ho mangiato la pizza. Sono andato a scuola.* — I ate the pizza. I went to school.

- Time expressions: che ora è, days, months, seasons.

> ***Es:** Che ora è? Sono le tre.* — What time is it? It's three o'clock.

- Quantifiers.

> ***Es:** Ho molto tempo. Ho poco denaro.* — I have a lot of time. I have little money.

## 6.2 — A2 (recap — full detail in Parts 2–4 of this guide)
This is the A2 level. One example per point — see Parts 2–4 for the complete explanations, endings, and irregular verb tables.

> ***Es:** Passato prossimo (avere/essere, full agreement rules) → Sono andata al mercato ieri.* — See Part 4.1–4.2
>
> ***Es:** Imperfetto → Da piccolo giocavo sempre nel parco.* — See Part 4.3
>
> ***Es:** Futuro semplice → Domani studierò tutto il giorno.* — See Part 4.4
>
> ***Es:** Condizionale presente → Vorrei un caffè, per favore.* — See Part 4.5
>
> ***Es:** Imperativo formale (Lei) → Mi scusi, può aiutarmi?* — Formal command
>
> ***Es:** Pronomi diretti/indiretti → Lo vedo. Gli parlo.* — I see him/it. I speak to him.
>
> ***Es:** Ne / ci (basic) → Ne ho due. Ci vado spesso.* — I have two (of them). I go there often.
>
> ***Es:** Preposizioni articolate → Vado al mercato. Torno dal lavoro.* — See Part 4.9
>
> ***Es:** Comparativi/superlativi (basic) → Marco è più alto di Luca. È il più bravo della classe.* — Marco is taller than Luca. He's the best in the class.
>
> ***Es:** Adverbs in -mente → Parla lentamente.* — See Part 3.2
>
> ***Es:** Pronomi possessivi → Questo è il mio, quello è il tuo.* — This is mine, that's yours.
>
> ***Es:** Presente progressivo → Sto mangiando.* — See Part 4.11
>
> ***Es:** Modal verbs in passato prossimo → Ho dovuto studiare.* — See Part 4.6
>
> ***Es:** Si impersonale (basic) → Qui si mangia bene.* — Here one eats well / the food is good here.

## 6.3 — B1 (what's next — explained in full, since it's new)
**The throughline: A1→A2 taught you to talk about TIME (past/future). A2→B1 teaches you to talk about IDEAS — opinion, doubt, hypothesis. The congiuntivo (subjunctive) is the big conceptual jump: it's a MOOD, not a tense, and nothing in A1/A2 works quite like it.**

**Congiuntivo — الوضع الافتراضي (mood of subjectivity, not a tense)**

Italian verbs have 'moods' (modi): Indicativo states FACTS you're certain of. Congiuntivo expresses OPINION, doubt, wish, emotion, or possibility — things that are subjective, not confirmed fact. This is the core logic: the same information changes form depending on how certain you are about it.

> ***Es:** So che Marco è italiano. (indicativo — certain fact)* — I KNOW Marco is Italian. — أعرف أنّ ماركو إيطالي (حقيقة مؤكدة)
>
> ***Es:** Penso che Marco sia italiano. (congiuntivo — opinion, not certain)* — I THINK Marco is Italian. — أعتقد أنّ ماركو إيطالي (رأي غير مؤكد)

*Formation logic — the vowels 'swap': -ARE verbs take -i endings (the opposite of their indicative -a); -ERE/-IRE verbs take -a endings (the opposite of their indicative -e/-o). Note io/tu/lui-lei share the SAME form — that's why Italians often say the subject pronoun explicitly with congiuntivo, to avoid ambiguity.*

**Congiuntivo Presente — formation:**

| **Persona** | **-ARE** | **-ERE** | **-IRE** |
|-------------|----------|----------|----------|
| io          | -i       | -a       | -a       |
| tu          | -i       | -a       | -a       |
| lui/lei     | -i       | -a       | -a       |
| noi         | -iamo    | -iamo    | -iamo    |
| voi         | -iate    | -iate    | -iate    |
| loro        | -ino     | -ano     | -ano     |

**Common irregulars:**

| **Infinito** | **io** | **tu** | **lui/lei** | **noi**  | **voi**  | **loro** |
|--------------|--------|--------|-------------|----------|----------|----------|
| essere       | sia    | sia    | sia         | siamo    | siate    | siano    |
| avere        | abbia  | abbia  | abbia       | abbiamo  | abbiate  | abbiano  |
| andare       | vada   | vada   | vada        | andiamo  | andiate  | vadano   |
| fare         | faccia | faccia | faccia      | facciamo | facciate | facciano |
| dire         | dica   | dica   | dica        | diciamo  | diciate  | dicano   |
| dovere       | debba  | debba  | debba       | dobbiamo | dobbiate | debbano  |
| potere       | possa  | possa  | possa       | possiamo | possiate | possano  |
| volere       | voglia | voglia | voglia      | vogliamo | vogliate | vogliano |
| venire       | venga  | venga  | venga       | veniamo  | veniate  | vengano  |
| sapere       | sappia | sappia | sappia      | sappiamo | sappiate | sappiano |
| stare        | stia   | stia   | stia        | stiamo   | stiate   | stiano   |
| dare         | dia    | dia    | dia         | diamo    | diate    | diano    |

**Congiuntivo is triggered by a verb/expression of opinion, doubt, emotion, or desire in the main clause + che:**

| **Espressione**  | **English**         | **العربية**  | **Esempio**                     |
|------------------|---------------------|--------------|---------------------------------|
| Penso che        | I think that        | أعتقد أنّ     | Penso che Marco sia italiano.   |
| Credo che        | I believe that      | أظنّ أنّ       | Credo che tu abbia ragione.     |
| Spero che        | I hope that         | أتمنى أنّ     | Spero che tu stia bene.         |
| È importante che | It's important that | من المهم أنْ  | È importante che tu studi.      |
| Voglio che       | I want (someone) to | أريد أنْ      | Voglio che tu venga alla festa. |
| Bisogna che      | It's necessary that | يجب أنْ       | Bisogna che partiamo presto.    |
| Mi sembra che    | It seems to me that | يبدو لي أنّ   | Mi sembra che piova.            |
| È possibile che  | It's possible that  | من الممكن أنْ | È possibile che arrivi tardi.   |

> ***Es:** Penso che Marco sia italiano.* — I think Marco is Italian. (sia = congiuntivo of essere) — أعتقد أنّ ماركو إيطالي
>
> ***Es:** Credo che tu abbia ragione.* — I believe you're right. — أظنّ أنّك على حق

**Important nuance — SAME subject vs DIFFERENT subject: if the subject doing the thinking and the subject of the second action are the SAME person, use di + infinito instead of che + congiuntivo. Congiuntivo is only for when the subjects are DIFFERENT.**

> ***Es:** Penso di avere ragione. (same subject: 'I think... I am right')* — I think I'm right. — أعتقد أنّني على حق (نفس الفاعل، لذا مصدر)
>
> ***Es:** Penso che tu abbia ragione. (different subjects: 'I think... YOU are right')* — I think you're right. — أعتقد أنّك على حق (فاعل مختلف، لذا مضارع شرطي)

**Congiuntivo Passato — opinions about a completed action:**

Formation: congiuntivo presente of avere/essere + participio passato. Used exactly like congiuntivo presente, but for something you believe already happened.

> ***Es:** Penso che abbia già mangiato.* — I think he has already eaten. — أعتقد أنّه أكل بالفعل
>
> ***Es:** Credo che siano partiti ieri.* — I believe they left yesterday. — أعتقد أنّهم غادروا أمس

**Periodo Ipotetico — إذا (if-clauses), now fully systematic**

You already met a simplified version of this at A2 ('se avessi tempo, studierei'). At B1 it becomes a 3-type system, each expressing a different degree of realism about the condition:

| **Tipo**                | **Struttura**                                        | **Esempio**                                | **English**                                                                          |
|-------------------------|------------------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| I — Reale               | Se + presente, + futuro/presente                     | Se piove, resto a casa.                    | If it rains, I'll stay home. — إذا أمطرت، سأبقى في البيت                             |
| II — Possibile          | Se + congiuntivo imperfetto, + condizionale presente | Se avessi tempo, studierei di più.         | If I had time, I would study more. — لو كان لدي وقت، لدرست أكثر                      |
| III — Irreale (passato) | Se + congiuntivo trapassato, + condizionale passato  | Se avessi studiato, avrei passato l'esame. | If I had studied, I would have passed the exam. — لو كنت درست، لكنت نجحت في الامتحان |

*Type I = a real, likely condition (both verbs can even stay in presente in casual speech). Type II = a condition that's possible but not currently true (right now, I don't have time). Type III = a condition about the PAST that did NOT happen — pure regret/speculation, since the past can't be changed.*

> ***Es:** Se ho tempo, ti chiamo. (very common, casual Type I)* — If I have time, I'll call you. — إذا كان لدي وقت، سأتصل بك

*Note: in everyday spoken Italian, many people use imperfetto indicativo instead of congiuntivo imperfetto in Type II ('se avevo tempo, studiavo') — grammatically incorrect but extremely common. Know the correct form for writing/exams; recognize the informal version when you hear it.*

**Trapassato Prossimo — 'the past before the past'**

Used when you're already talking about the past, and need to refer to something that happened EVEN EARLIER than that — the same relationship Trapassato has to Passato Prossimo that Passato Prossimo has to 'now'.

Formation: avevo/eri (imperfetto of avere/essere) + participio passato.

> ***Es:** Avevo già mangiato quando sei arrivato.* — I had already eaten when you arrived. — كنت قد أكلتُ بالفعل عندما وصلتَ
>
> ***Es:** Quando siamo arrivati, il film era già iniziato.* — When we arrived, the film had already started. — عندما وصلنا، كان الفيلم قد بدأ بالفعل

**Passato Remoto — recognition mainly (literature, formal narration, some regional speech)**

A completed-past tense like passato prossimo, but used for events felt as psychologically distant/disconnected from now — history, fairy tales, biographies. In much of central/northern Italy it's rare in speech (replaced by passato prossimo); in the south (e.g. Sicily) it's still common even in daily conversation.

> ***Es:** Dante nacque nel 1265.* — Dante was born in 1265. — وُلد دانتي عام 1265 (تُقرأ أكثر مما تُستخدم في الكلام)

**Pronomi Combinati — stacking indirect + direct object pronouns**

When a sentence has BOTH an indirect object (to whom) and a direct object (what), Italian combines them into one unit: indirect object pronoun comes FIRST, direct object SECOND, and mi/ti/ci/vi change their final -i to -e before lo/la/li/le/ne (mi→me, ti→te, ci→ce, vi→ve). Gli and le both become glie- and fuse into one word with the direct pronoun.

| **Combinazione**     | **Forma**                         | **Esempio**                 | **English**                                          |
|----------------------|-----------------------------------|-----------------------------|------------------------------------------------------|
| mi + lo/la/li/le     | me lo / me la / me li / me le     | Me lo presti?               | Will you lend it to me? — هل تُعيره لي؟               |
| ti + lo/la/li/le     | te lo / te la / te li / te le     | Te lo dico domani.          | I'll tell it to you tomorrow. — سأخبرك به غداً        |
| gli/le + lo/la/li/le | glielo / gliela / glieli / gliele | Glielo do io.               | I'll give it to him/her. — سأعطيه له/لها             |
| ci + lo/la/li/le     | ce lo / ce la / ce li / ce le     | Ce lo spiega il professore. | The teacher explains it to us. — يشرحه لنا الأستاذ   |
| vi + lo/la/li/le     | ve lo / ve la / ve li / ve le     | Ve lo mando stasera.        | I'll send it to you tonight. — سأرسله لكم هذا المساء |

*Order to remember: chi riceve (who receives) + cosa (what) — indiretto + diretto, always in that order, never reversed.*

**Comparativi e superlativi irregolari**

You learned the regular pattern at A2 (più...di / il più...). Four common adjectives have a SHORT irregular form used alongside (sometimes instead of) the regular one:

| **Aggettivo**                | **Comparativo**                 | **Superlativo**                            |
|------------------------------|---------------------------------|--------------------------------------------|
| buono (good — جيد)           | migliore (better — أفضل)        | il migliore (the best — الأفضل)            |
| cattivo (bad — سيء)          | peggiore (worse — أسوأ)         | il peggiore (the worst — الأسوأ)           |
| grande (big/old — كبير)      | maggiore (bigger/older — أكبر)  | il maggiore (the biggest/oldest — الأكبر)  |
| piccolo (small/young — صغير) | minore (smaller/younger — أصغر) | il minore (the smallest/youngest — الأصغر) |

> ***Es:** Questo ristorante è migliore di quello.* — This restaurant is better than that one. — هذا المطعم أفضل من ذاك

**Discorso Indiretto — reported speech, with tense-shifting**

When you report what someone else said using 'ha detto che...', the original tense shifts BACK one step in time — because it's now embedded in a past-tense sentence:

| **Cambio**                             | **Esempio**                                 | **English**                         |
|----------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------|
| Presente → Imperfetto                  | "Sono stanco" → ha detto che era stanco     | "I'm tired" → he said he was tired  |
| Passato Prossimo → Trapassato Prossimo | "Ho mangiato" → ha detto che aveva mangiato | "I ate" → he said he had eaten      |
| Futuro → Condizionale Passato          | "Verrò" → ha detto che sarebbe venuto       | "I'll come" → he said he would come |

*This mirrors English's own reported-speech backshift ('I'm tired' → 'he said he WAS tired'), so the logic should feel familiar even if the forms are new.*

**Subordinate clauses that require congiuntivo**

Certain connecting words grammatically force congiuntivo in the clause that follows them, regardless of how certain the fact is — this is a fixed rule to memorize per connector, not a logic you can derive:

| **Connettivo**   | **English**        | **العربية**    | **Esempio**                             |
|------------------|--------------------|----------------|-----------------------------------------|
| sebbene / benché | although           | رغمَ أنّ / مع أنّ | Benché sia stanco, continuo a lavorare. |
| nonostante       | despite / although | بالرغم من      | Nonostante piova, usciamo lo stesso.    |
| affinché         | so that            | لكي / حتى      | Ti spiego affinché tu capisca bene.     |
| a meno che (non) | unless             | إلا إذا        | Vengo alla festa a meno che non piova.  |
| prima che        | before             | قبل أنْ         | Finiamo il lavoro prima che arrivino.   |

**Forma Passiva / Si Passivante — shifting focus from WHO acts to WHAT happens**

Passive voice formation: essere (in the needed tense) + participio passato, which agrees with the subject like with essere-verbs in passato prossimo. Used when the agent is unknown, unimportant, or you simply want to emphasize the object instead.

> ***Es:** La lettera è stata scritta da Marco.* — The letter was written by Marco. — كُتبت الرسالة من قبل ماركو

Si passivante is a common shortcut for general/impersonal statements — especially menus, signs, instructions.

> ***Es:** Si vendono case.* — Houses are sold / for sale. — تُباع منازل هنا (إعلان عام)
>
> ***Es:** Qui si parla inglese.* — English is spoken here. — هنا تُتحدث الإنجليزية

**Imperativo + pronomi combinati — pronouns ATTACH to the end of informal commands**

> ***Es:** Dammelo! (da' + mi + lo, fused into one word)* — Give it to me! — أعطني إياه!
>
> ***Es:** Dimmelo! (di' + mi + lo)* — Tell it to me! — قله لي!

**Gerundio Passato — a completed action that precedes another**

Formation: avendo/essendo (gerund of avere/essere) + participio passato. Used to compress 'after having done X, I did Y' into one clause.

> ***Es:** Avendo finito il lavoro, sono uscito.* — Having finished the work, I went out. — بعد أن أنهيتُ العمل، خرجتُ

*Practical note: congiuntivo and periodo ipotetico will likely be the two biggest new challenges — they're conceptually different from anything in A1/A2, so it's worth spending extra time on them early.*

# A2 · PART 7 — SLANG & EVERYDAY EXPRESSIONS
Textbook Italian and spoken Italian aren't the same thing — these are the fillers and reactions you'll actually hear from classmates, colleagues, and on the street. Most are casual; a couple are flagged as friends-only.

| **Italiano**                   | **English**                                                           | **العربية**               | **Nota**                                                                        |
|--------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Che figata!                    | How cool! / Awesome!                                                  | رائع! / كول!              | Very common, all ages                                                           |
| Boh!                           | I dunno (shrug)                                                       | ما أدري / مش عارف         | Extremely common, casual                                                        |
| Magari!                        | I wish! / If only!                                                    | يا ريت!                   | Common, hopeful tone                                                            |
| Dai!                           | Come on!                                                              | يلا!                      | Encouraging or impatient, very frequent                                         |
| Non vedo l'ora!                | I can't wait!                                                         | ما أقدر أستنى / متحمس جداً | Excitement about something coming up                                            |
| In bocca al lupo!              | Good luck! (lit. 'in the wolf's mouth')                               | بالتوفيق!                 | Reply: 'Crepi!' — never say 'buona fortuna' among Italians, considered bad luck |
| Figurati! / Si figuri (formal) | No worries! / Don't mention it!                                       | عادي! / لا داعي للشكر     | Response to thanks or apology                                                   |
| Meno male!                     | Thank goodness! / Phew!                                               | الحمد لله!                | Relief                                                                          |
| Che casino!                    | What a mess! / What chaos!                                            | يا لها من فوضى!           | Very common, mildly informal                                                    |
| Sbrigati!                      | Hurry up!                                                             | أسرع!                     | Informal command                                                                |
| Non ci credo!                  | No way! / I don't believe it!                                         | ما أصدق!                  | Surprise                                                                        |
| Vabbè                          | OK then / whatever (short for va bene)                                | ماشي / حسناً               | Very casual, shortened speech                                                   |
| Ci sto!                        | I'm in! / Count me in!                                                | أنا معكم! / موافق!        | Agreeing to a plan                                                              |
| Fico / Figo                    | cool (adjective)                                                      | كول / رائع                | Slang adjective, all ages                                                       |
| Un attimo                      | Just a moment                                                         | لحظة واحدة                | Very common, polite pause                                                       |
| Mica male!                     | Not bad at all!                                                       | مش سيء أبداً!              | Mild compliment                                                                 |
| Sul serio?                     | Seriously? / For real?                                                | بجد؟                      | Surprise/disbelief                                                              |
| Che palle!                     | What a drag! / So annoying! (mildly crude, very common among friends) | يا للإزعاج!               | Informal, use only with close friends — not in formal/work settings             |

# A2 · PART 8 — SAMPLE CONVERSATIONS (dialoghi)
Four realistic dialogues — read them out loud, then try covering the English/Arabic columns and reconstructing the Italian from memory. The weekend one doubles as extra oral-exam practice.

## 8.1 Al bar — ordering coffee (A1/A2)
|          | **Italiano**                                                | **English**                                                  | **العربية**                                 |
|----------|-------------------------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------|
| Barista: | Buongiorno! Cosa prende?                                    | Good morning! What'll you have?                              | صباح الخير! ماذا تريد؟                      |
| Tu:      | Buongiorno, vorrei un cappuccino e un cornetto, per favore. | Good morning, I'd like a cappuccino and a croissant, please. | صباح الخير، أريد كابتشينو وكرواسون من فضلك. |
| Barista: | Subito! Da bere niente altro?                               | Right away! Nothing else to drink?                           | حالاً! لا شيء آخر للشرب؟                     |
| Tu:      | No, grazie, va bene così.                                   | No thanks, that's all.                                       | لا شكراً، هذا يكفي.                          |
| Barista: | Ecco a lei. Sono tre euro.                                  | Here you go. That's three euros.                             | تفضل. ثلاثة يورو.                           |
| Tu:      | Grazie mille, arrivederci!                                  | Thank you very much, goodbye!                                | شكراً جزيلاً، إلى اللقاء!                     |
| Barista: | Arrivederci, buona giornata!                                | Goodbye, have a nice day!                                    | إلى اللقاء، يوماً سعيداً!                     |

## 8.2 Il weekend scorso — talking about last weekend (A2)
|     | **Italiano**                                                              | **English**                                               | **العربية**                                             |
|-----|---------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------|
| A:  | Ciao! Cosa hai fatto il weekend scorso?                                   | Hi! What did you do last weekend?                         | أهلاً! ماذا فعلت في نهاية الأسبوع الماضية؟               |
| B:  | Ciao! Sabato sono andato al mare con la mia famiglia. E tu?               | Hi! Saturday I went to the sea with my family. And you?   | أهلاً! يوم السبت ذهبت إلى البحر مع عائلتي. وأنت؟         |
| A:  | Io sono rimasto a casa, ero un po' stanco. Ho studiato per l'esame.       | I stayed home, I was a bit tired. I studied for the exam. | أنا بقيت في البيت، كنت متعباً قليلاً. درست للامتحان.      |
| B:  | Ah, in bocca al lupo per l'esame!                                         | Oh, good luck with the exam!                              | آه، بالتوفيق في الامتحان!                               |
| A:  | Grazie! Domenica invece cosa hai fatto?                                   | Thanks! What did you do on Sunday?                        | شكراً! ماذا فعلت يوم الأحد؟                              |
| B:  | Domenica ho dormito fino a tardi e poi ho guardato un film con gli amici. | Sunday I slept in and then watched a movie with friends.  | يوم الأحد نمت حتى وقت متأخر ثم شاهدت فيلماً مع الأصدقاء. |

## 8.3 Chiedere indicazioni — asking for directions (A2)
|           | **Italiano**                                                         | **English**                                                               | **العربية**                                                     |
|-----------|----------------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------|
| Tu:       | Scusi, mi può dire dov'è la stazione?                                | Excuse me, can you tell me where the station is?                          | عفواً، هل يمكنك أن تخبرني أين المحطة؟                            |
| Passante: | Certo! Vada dritto per questa strada, poi giri a destra al semaforo. | Sure! Go straight down this street, then turn right at the traffic light. | بالتأكيد! سِر مستقيماً في هذا الشارع، ثم انعطف يميناً عند الإشارة. |
| Tu:       | Dritto e poi a destra, ho capito. È lontano?                         | Straight then right, got it. Is it far?                                   | مستقيم ثم يمين، فهمت. هل هي بعيدة؟                              |
| Passante: | No, sono circa cinque minuti a piedi.                                | No, it's about five minutes on foot.                                      | لا، حوالي خمس دقائق سيراً على الأقدام.                           |
| Tu:       | Grazie mille!                                                        | Thank you very much!                                                      | شكراً جزيلاً!                                                     |
| Passante: | Prego, buona giornata!                                               | You're welcome, have a nice day!                                          | عفواً، يوماً سعيداً!                                               |

## 8.4 Dare un'opinione — giving an opinion on a film (B1, uses congiuntivo)
|     | **Italiano**                                                    | **English**                                                    | **العربية**                                         |
|-----|-----------------------------------------------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------|
| A:  | Hai visto l'ultimo film di Sorrentino?                          | Have you seen Sorrentino's latest film?                        | هل شاهدت آخر فيلم لسورينتينو؟                       |
| B:  | Sì! Penso che sia un capolavoro, sinceramente.                  | Yes! I honestly think it's a masterpiece.                      | نعم! أعتقد بصراحة أنه تحفة فنية.                    |
| A:  | Davvero? A me non è piaciuto tanto. Credo che sia troppo lento. | Really? I didn't like it that much. I think it's too slow.     | حقاً؟ أنا لم يعجبني كثيراً. أعتقد أنه بطيء جداً.       |
| B:  | Può darsi, ma secondo me la fotografia è incredibile.           | Maybe, but in my opinion the cinematography is incredible.     | ربما، لكن في رأيي التصوير السينمائي رائع.           |
| A:  | Su questo hai ragione. Magari lo rivedo con più attenzione.     | You're right about that. Maybe I'll rewatch it more carefully. | أنت محق في هذا. ربما سأشاهده مرة أخرى بانتباه أكبر. |

*Notice: penso che sia, credo che sia — congiuntivo after opinion verbs, exactly as explained in Part 6.3. Secondo me and può darsi are two more very natural ways to give an opinion without needing congiuntivo at all.*

# A2 · PART 9 — A2 VOCABULARY (with Arabic)
All 151 words from your A2 vocabulary pack, grouped by topic. Each entry has the English meaning, the Arabic, and your original example sentence with its translation. Cover the right-hand columns and test yourself.

## 9.1 Time expressions — past (tempi del passato)
*Clue words that tell you to use Passato Prossimo (a finished moment) — see also the clue-word table in Part 4.10.*

| **Italiano**            | **English**          | **العربية**     | **Esempio**                                                                 |
|-------------------------|----------------------|----------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **ieri**                | yesterday            | أمس             | *Ieri ho mangiato una pizza.* / Yesterday I had a pizza.                    |
| **ieri sera**           | yesterday evening    | مساء أمس        | *Ieri sera sono andato al cinema.* / Last night I went to the cinema.       |
| **ieri notte**          | last night           | ليلة أمس        | *Ieri notte ho dormito bene.* / Last night I slept well.                    |
| **l'altro ieri**        | day before yesterday | أول أمس         | *L'altro ieri ho incontrato Marco.* / The day before yesterday I met Marco. |
| **la scorsa settimana** | last week            | الأسبوع الماضي  | *La scorsa settimana ho visitato Firenze.* / Last week I visited Florence.  |
| **il mese scorso**      | last month           | الشهر الماضي    | *Il mese scorso ho cambiato lavoro.* / Last month I changed jobs.           |
| **l'anno scorso**       | last year            | العام الماضي    | *L'anno scorso sono stato in Italia.* / Last year I was in Italy.           |
| **qualche giorno fa**   | a few days ago       | قبل بضعة أيام   | *Qualche giorno fa ho visto Maria.* / A few days ago I saw Maria.           |
| **poco fa**             | a little while ago   | منذ قليل        | *Poco fa ho finito di lavorare.* / I just finished working.                 |
| **un'ora fa**           | an hour ago          | قبل ساعة        | *Un'ora fa ero ancora a casa.* / An hour ago I was still at home.           |
| **appena**              | just                 | للتو            | *Ho appena finito.* / I just finished.                                      |
| **già**                 | already              | بالفعل / من قبل | *Hai già mangiato?* / Have you eaten already?                               |
| **ancora**              | yet/still            | ما زال / بعد    | *Non ho ancora finito.* / I haven't finished yet.                           |
| **mai**                 | never                | أبداً            | *Non sono mai stato a Roma.* / I have never been to Rome.                   |
| **sempre**              | always               | دائماً           | *Sono sempre stato curioso.* / I have always been curious.                  |

## 9.2 Time expressions — future
*Clue words for Futuro Semplice, or Presente used for a planned future (Domani vado al cinema).*

| **Italiano**              | **English**        | **العربية**    | **Esempio**                                                                     |
|---------------------------|--------------------|---------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **domani**                | tomorrow           | غداً            | *Domani vado al cinema.* / Tomorrow I'm going to the cinema.                    |
| **domani sera**           | tomorrow evening   | مساء الغد      | *Domani sera ceno fuori.* / Tomorrow evening I'm having dinner out.             |
| **dopodomani**            | day after tomorrow | بعد غد         | *Dopodomani parto per Roma.* / The day after tomorrow I'm leaving for Rome.     |
| **la prossima settimana** | next week          | الأسبوع القادم | *La prossima settimana inizio un corso.* / Next week I start a course.          |
| **il mese prossimo**      | next month         | الشهر القادم   | *Il mese prossimo cambio casa.* / Next month I'm moving house.                  |
| **l'anno prossimo**       | next year          | العام القادم   | *L'anno prossimo andrò in Italia.* / Next year I will go to Italy.              |
| **tra poco**              | soon               | بعد قليل       | *Arrivo tra poco.* / I'll be there shortly.                                     |
| **presto**                | soon               | قريباً          | *Ci vediamo presto!* / See you soon!                                            |
| **più tardi**             | later              | لاحقاً          | *Ti chiamo più tardi.* / I'll call you later.                                   |
| **dopo**                  | after              | بعد            | *Dopo pranzo vado a casa.* / After lunch I'm going home.                        |
| **prima**                 | before             | قبل            | *Prima di uscire, chiudo le finestre.* / Before going out, I close the windows. |

## 9.3 Describing experiences
*Use with È stato/a (whole event, finished) or era (description of how it was).*

| **Italiano**        | **English**   | **العربية**               | **Esempio**                                                                    |
|---------------------|---------------|--------------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **fantastico/a**    | fantastic     | رائع                      | *È stato un viaggio fantastico.* / It was a fantastic trip.                    |
| **indimenticabile** | unforgettable | لا يُنسى                   | *È stata un'esperienza indimenticabile.* / It was an unforgettable experience. |
| **meraviglioso/a**  | wonderful     | مذهل / رائع               | *La vista era meravigliosa.* / The view was wonderful.                         |
| **stupendo/a**      | amazing       | مدهش                      | *Il cibo era stupendo.* / The food was amazing.                                |
| **emozionante**     | exciting      | مثير                      | *È stato molto emozionante.* / It was very exciting.                           |
| **rilassante**      | relaxing      | مريح / يبعث على الاسترخاء | *La vacanza è stata rilassante.* / The holiday was relaxing.                   |
| **piacevole**       | pleasant      | ممتع / لطيف               | *È stata una serata piacevole.* / It was a pleasant evening.                   |
| **divertente**      | fun           | مسلٍّ / ممتع                | *La festa è stata molto divertente.* / The party was a lot of fun.             |
| **interessante**    | interesting   | مثير للاهتمام             | *Il museo era interessante.* / The museum was interesting.                     |
| **speciale**        | special       | مميز                      | *È stato un momento speciale.* / It was a special moment.                      |
| **terribile**       | terrible      | فظيع                      | *Il servizio era terribile.* / The service was terrible.                       |
| **deludente**       | disappointing | مخيّب للآمال               | *Il film è stato deludente.* / The movie was disappointing.                    |
| **noioso/a**        | boring        | ممل                       | *La conferenza era noiosa.* / The conference was boring.                       |
| **stressante**      | stressful     | مرهق / مُجهد               | *Il lavoro era stressante.* / The job was stressful.                           |
| **difficile**       | difficult     | صعب                       | *È stata una situazione difficile.* / It was a difficult situation.            |
| **spiacevole**      | unpleasant    | غير سار                   | *È stata un'esperienza spiacevole.* / It was an unpleasant experience.         |
| **frustrante**      | frustrating   | محبط                      | *È stato frustrante aspettare.* / It was frustrating to wait.                  |

## 9.4 Opinions & judgments
*Remember: penso che / credo che take the congiuntivo (Part 6.3). Secondo me / per me do not.*

| **Italiano**           | **English**       | **العربية**           | **Esempio**                                                       |
|------------------------|-------------------|----------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **secondo me**         | in my opinion     | في رأيي               | *Secondo me, hai ragione.* / In my opinion, you are right.        |
| **a mio parere**       | in my opinion     | في رأيي (رسمي)        | *A mio parere, è troppo caro.* / In my view, it is too expensive. |
| **per me**             | for me            | بالنسبة لي            | *Per me, va bene così.* / For me, that's fine as it is.           |
| **penso che**          | I think that      | أظن أن                | *Penso che sia una buona idea.* / I think it's a good idea.       |
| **credo che**          | I believe that    | أعتقد أن              | *Credo che tu abbia ragione.* / I believe you are right.          |
| **sono d'accordo**     | I agree           | أوافق                 | *Sono d'accordo con te.* / I agree with you.                      |
| **hai ragione**        | you're right      | أنت محق               | *Hai assolutamente ragione!* / You are absolutely right!          |
| **esatto**             | exactly           | بالضبط                | *Esatto, è così!* / Exactly, that's right!                        |
| **non sono d'accordo** | I don't agree     | لا أوافق              | *Non sono d'accordo.* / I do not agree.                           |
| **non penso**          | I don't think     | لا أظن                | *Non penso sia così.* / I don't think that's the case.            |
| **non credo**          | I don't believe   | لا أعتقد              | *Non credo che sia vero.* / I don't believe it's true.            |
| **vedo diversamente**  | I see differently | أرى الأمور بشكل مختلف | *Vedo le cose diversamente.* / I see things differently.          |

## 9.5 Comparisons & qualities
*Grammar for these is in Part 4 and in the A2 Comparisons section: più … di, meno … di, tanto … quanto.*

| **Italiano**        | **English**  | **العربية**  | **Esempio**                                                                |
|---------------------|--------------|-------------------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **più... di**       | more... than | أكثر ... من  | *Roma è più grande di Milano.* / Rome is larger than Milan.                |
| **meno... di**      | less... than | أقل ... من   | *Questo è meno caro di quello.* / This is cheaper than that.               |
| **tanto... quanto** | as... as     | بقدر ... مثل | *È tanto bravo quanto te.* / He is as good as you.                         |
| **così... come**    | as... as     | بنفس ... مثل | *Non sono così alto come te.* / I am not as tall as you.                   |
| **migliore**        | better       | أفضل         | *Questo è migliore di quello.* / This is better than that.                 |
| **peggiore**        | worse        | أسوأ         | *La situazione è peggiore di prima.* / The situation is worse than before. |
| **qualità**         | quality      | الجودة       | *La qualità è eccellente.* / The quality is excellent.                     |
| **vantaggio**       | advantage    | ميزة         | *Il vantaggio è che...* / The advantage is that...                         |
| **svantaggio**      | disadvantage | عيب          | *Lo svantaggio è che...* / The disadvantage is that...                     |
| **punto di forza**  | strength     | نقطة قوة     | *Il punto di forza è...* / The strong point is...                          |
| **punto debole**    | weakness     | نقطة ضعف     | *Il punto debole è...* / The weak point is...                              |

## 9.6 Places & locations
*Great for the 'describe your city' speaking prompts (Part 10, Days 30 and 39).*

| **Italiano**       | **English**     | **العربية**     | **Esempio**                                                           |
|--------------------|-----------------|----------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **centro storico** | historic center | المركز التاريخي | *Il centro storico è bellissimo.* / The historic center is beautiful. |
| **quartiere**      | neighborhood    | حي              | *Abito in un quartiere tranquillo.* / I live in a quiet neighborhood. |
| **periferia**      | outskirts       | الضواحي         | *La periferia è più economica.* / The suburbs are cheaper.            |
| **piazza**         | square          | ساحة            | *Ci vediamo in piazza.* / See you in the square.                      |
| **via**            | street          | شارع            | *Abito in via Roma.* / I live on Via Roma.                            |
| **vicolo**         | alley           | زقاق            | *I vicoli sono stretti.* / The alleys are narrow.                     |
| **monumento**      | monument        | نصب تذكاري      | *Il monumento è molto antico.* / The monument is very old.            |
| **chiesa**         | church          | كنيسة           | *La chiesa è in centro.* / The church is in the center.               |
| **museo**          | museum          | متحف            | *Il museo è chiuso il lunedì.* / The museum is closed on Mondays.     |
| **teatro**         | theater         | مسرح            | *Andiamo a teatro stasera?* / Shall we go to the theater tonight?     |
| **parco**          | park            | حديقة           | *Il parco è molto grande.* / The park is very large.                  |
| **ponte**          | bridge          | جسر             | *Il ponte è molto famoso.* / The bridge is very famous.               |
| **affollato/a**    | crowded         | مزدحم           | *Il centro è sempre affollato.* / The center is always crowded.       |
| **tranquillo/a**   | quiet           | هادئ            | *È un posto molto tranquillo.* / It is a very quiet place.            |
| **vivace**         | lively          | نابض بالحياة    | *La città è molto vivace.* / The city is very lively.                 |
| **moderno/a**      | modern          | حديث            | *È un quartiere moderno.* / It's a modern neighborhood.               |
| **antico/a**       | ancient         | قديم / أثري     | *Ci sono molti edifici antichi.* / There are many ancient buildings.  |
| **turistico/a**    | touristy        | سياحي           | *È una zona molto turistica.* / It's a very touristy area.            |

## 9.7 Emotions & feelings
*Adjectives agree with the speaker: sono stanco (m) / sono stanca (f).*

| **Italiano**      | **English**  | **العربية**       | **Esempio**                                                             |
|-------------------|--------------|------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **felice**        | happy        | سعيد              | *Sono molto felice.* / I am very happy.                                 |
| **contento/a**    | content      | راضٍ / مسرور       | *Sono contento di vederti.* / I am glad to see you.                     |
| **entusiasta**    | enthusiastic | متحمس             | *Sono entusiasta del progetto.* / I am excited about the project.       |
| **emozionato/a**  | excited      | متحمس / منفعل     | *Sono emozionato per il viaggio.* / I am excited for the trip.          |
| **orgoglioso/a**  | proud        | فخور              | *Sono orgoglioso di te.* / I am proud of you.                           |
| **soddisfatto/a** | satisfied    | راضٍ               | *Sono soddisfatto del risultato.* / I am satisfied with the result.     |
| **rilassato/a**   | relaxed      | مرتاح             | *Mi sento rilassato.* / I feel relaxed.                                 |
| **triste**        | sad          | حزين              | *Sono triste per la notizia.* / I am sad about the news.                |
| **arrabbiato/a**  | angry        | غاضب              | *Sono arrabbiato con te.* / I am angry with you.                        |
| **deluso/a**      | disappointed | مُحبَط / خائب الأمل | *Sono deluso dal risultato.* / I am disappointed with the result.       |
| **frustrato/a**   | frustrated   | محبط              | *Mi sento frustrato.* / I feel frustrated.                              |
| **preoccupato/a** | worried      | قلق               | *Sono preoccupato per l'esame.* / I am worried about the exam.          |
| **ansioso/a**     | anxious      | قلق / متوتر       | *Sono ansioso per domani.* / I am anxious for tomorrow.                 |
| **stressato/a**   | stressed     | مضغوط             | *Sono molto stressato ultimamente.* / I have been very stressed lately. |
| **stanco/a**      | tired        | متعب              | *Sono stanco di tutto questo.* / I am tired of all this.                |
| **annoiato/a**    | bored        | ضجر / ملول        | *Mi sento annoiato.* / I feel bored.                                    |
| **mi sento**      | I feel       | أشعر              | *Mi sento bene oggi.* / I feel good today.                              |
| **ho paura**      | I'm afraid   | أخاف / أنا خائف   | *Ho paura di sbagliare.* / I am afraid of making a mistake.             |

## 9.8 Activities & hobbies
*Infinitives work as nouns here: Leggere è il mio hobby.*

| **Italiano**         | **English**          | **العربية**           | **Esempio**                                                                |
|----------------------|----------------------|----------------------------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **hobby**            | hobby                | هواية                 | *Il mio hobby è la fotografia.* / My hobby is photography.                 |
| **passatempo**       | pastime              | تسلية                 | *Il mio passatempo preferito è leggere.* / My favorite pastime is reading. |
| **interesse**        | interest             | اهتمام                | *Ho molti interessi.* / I have many interests.                             |
| **passione**         | passion              | شغف                   | *La mia passione è la musica.* / My passion is music.                      |
| **leggere**          | reading              | القراءة               | *Leggere è il mio hobby.* / Reading is my hobby.                           |
| **scrivere**         | writing              | الكتابة               | *Mi piace scrivere.* / I like to write.                                    |
| **fotografare**      | photography          | التصوير               | *Fotografare è la mia passione.* / Photography is my passion.              |
| **cucinare**         | cooking              | الطبخ                 | *Cucinare mi rilassa.* / Cooking relaxes me.                               |
| **viaggiare**        | traveling            | السفر                 | *Viaggiare è il mio sogno.* / Traveling is my dream.                       |
| **fare sport**       | doing sports         | ممارسة الرياضة        | *Fare sport mi fa bene.* / Doing sports is good for me.                    |
| **suonare**          | playing (instrument) | العزف                 | *Suono il piano.* / I play the piano.                                      |
| **dipingere**        | painting             | الرسم                 | *Dipingere è creativo.* / Painting is creative.                            |
| **guardare film**    | watching movies      | مشاهدة الأفلام        | *Guardare film è divertente.* / Watching movies is fun.                    |
| **ascoltare musica** | listening to music   | الاستماع إلى الموسيقى | *Ascoltare musica mi rilassa.* / Listening to music relaxes me.            |

## 9.9 Work & studies
*Note: collega → colleghi (plural). Studiare = to study.*

| **Italiano**   | **English**         | **العربية**  | **Esempio**                                                      |
|----------------|---------------------|-------------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **lavoro**     | work/job            | عمل / وظيفة  | *Il mio lavoro è interessante.* / My job is interesting.         |
| **ufficio**    | office              | مكتب         | *Lavoro in un ufficio.* / I work in an office.                   |
| **collega**    | colleague           | زميل         | *I miei colleghi sono simpatici.* / My colleagues are nice.      |
| **capo**       | boss                | مدير / رئيس  | *Il mio capo è molto esigente.* / My boss is very demanding.     |
| **riunione**   | meeting             | اجتماع       | *Ho una riunione domani.* / I have a meeting tomorrow.           |
| **progetto**   | project             | مشروع        | *Sto lavorando su un progetto.* / I am working on a project.     |
| **scadenza**   | deadline            | موعد نهائي   | *La scadenza è venerdì.* / The deadline is Friday.               |
| **stipendio**  | salary              | راتب         | *Lo stipendio è buono.* / The salary is good.                    |
| **carriera**   | career              | مسيرة مهنية  | *La mia carriera è importante.* / My career is important.        |
| **promozione** | promotion           | ترقية        | *Ho ricevuto una promozione.* / I received a promotion.          |
| **studiare**   | to study            | يدرس         | *Studio italiano ogni giorno.* / I study Italian every day.      |
| **esame**      | exam                | امتحان       | *Ho un esame la prossima settimana.* / I have an exam next week. |
| **compito**    | homework/assignment | واجب         | *Devo fare i compiti.* / I need to do my homework.               |
| **lezione**    | lesson              | درس          | *La lezione è interessante.* / The lesson is interesting.        |
| **professore** | professor           | أستاذ        | *Il professore è molto bravo.* / The professor is very good.     |
| **università** | university          | جامعة        | *Studio all'università.* / I study at the university.            |
| **laurea**     | degree              | شهادة جامعية | *Sto prendendo una laurea.* / I am getting a degree.             |
| **voto**       | grade               | درجة / علامة | *Ho preso un buon voto.* / I got a good grade.                   |

## 9.10 Travel & transportation
*Useful for stations, tickets and airport situations.*

| **Italiano**         | **English** | **العربية** | **Esempio**                                                           |
|----------------------|-------------|------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------|
| **viaggio**          | trip        | رحلة        | *Il viaggio è stato lungo.* / The trip was long.                      |
| **vacanza**          | vacation    | عطلة        | *Le vacanze sono finite.* / The holidays are over.                    |
| **destinazione**     | destination | وجهة        | *La destinazione è Roma.* / The destination is Rome.                  |
| **partenza**         | departure   | مغادرة      | *La partenza è alle otto.* / The departure is at eight.               |
| **arrivo**           | arrival     | وصول        | *L'arrivo è previsto per le dieci.* / The arrival is expected at ten. |
| **bagaglio**         | luggage     | أمتعة       | *Ho perso il bagaglio.* / I lost my luggage.                          |
| **valigia**          | suitcase    | حقيبة سفر   | *La valigia è pesante.* / The suitcase is heavy.                      |
| **passaporto**       | passport    | جواز سفر    | *Ho dimenticato il passaporto.* / I forgot my passport.               |
| **prenotazione**     | reservation | حجز         | *Ho una prenotazione.* / I have a reservation.                        |
| **biglietto**        | ticket      | تذكرة       | *Ho comprato il biglietto.* / I bought the ticket.                    |
| **andata e ritorno** | round trip  | ذهاب وإياب  | *Un biglietto andata e ritorno.* / A round-trip ticket.               |
